Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the energy object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 975. The moduli space has dimension at most 728 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to energy theory, we decompose the problem into 975 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 728. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 218.5 (Spectral Property)
